# Polity & Governance → Institutions, Regulation & Governance → Non-Constitutional & Statutory Bodies — Source Pack v01

**Status:** PASS_WITH_CORRECTIONS  
**taxonomy_id:** `prelims-polity-and-governance-institutions-regulation-and-governance-non-constitutional-and-statutory-bodies`

## 1. Scope
- **Includes:** NITI Aayog; erstwhile Planning Commission; National Development Council (NDC); constitutional vs statutory vs executive/non-statutory status; Lokpal; Inter-State Council; Zonal Councils; body-status distinctions directly required by 2023/2025 UPSC PYQs. Taxonomy specifically owns NITI/Planning bodies, NDC, Lokpal and regional councils. PRE-POL__TAXONOMY_GATEWAY
- **Excludes:** detailed regulators/agencies owned by sibling node; dynamic Lokpal functioning/current office-holders; current NITI indices/rankings; scheme/portal trivia; sectoral economic planning mechanisms.
- **UPSC depth:** source-of-creation classification; “how many constitutional?” elimination; NDC/Planning/NITI institutional distinctions; Article 263 vs statutory Zonal Councils; Lokpal Act fine print.
- **UPPCS depth:** direct factual recall—first NITI Vice-Chairman; Planning Commission legacy; Sarkaria Commission → ISC; NITI institutional linkage; Lokpal Selection Committee.

## 2. PYQ Demand Map

### UPSC

**A. Constitutional / statutory / executive status**
- `UPSC-CSE-2013-GS1-Q019` — NDC + Planning Commission + Zonal Councils: Constitution mention/status.
- `UPSC-CSE-2023-GS1-Q035` — NCBC vs NHRC vs Law Commission vs NCDRC: count constitutional bodies.
- `UPSC-CSE-2025-GS1-Q053` — Inter-State Council vs National Security Council vs Zonal Councils: count Constitution-established bodies.

**B. Planning architecture: Planning Commission → NITI; NDC**
- `UPSC-CSE-2013-GS1-Q069` — NDC ≠ organ of Planning Commission; planning constitutional context.
- `UPSC-CSE-2013-GS1-Q098` — NDC composition.
- `UPSC-CSE-2014-GS1-Q057` — institutions associated with national planning. **Canonical answer record requires correction; see §7.**
- `UPSC-CSE-2015-GS1-Q042` — NITI replaced Planning Commission.

**C. Lokpal legal fine print**
- `UPSC-CSE-2025-GS1-Q098` — territorial application; eligibility/disqualifications; Chairperson qualification; age ≥45; jurisdiction over sitting PM.

**Semantic exclusions → sibling/other nodes**
- `UPSC-CSE-2016-GS1-Q056` FSDC–NITI matching.
- `UPSC-CSE-2017-GS1-Q055` NIIF–NITI matching.
- `UPSC-CSE-2019-GS1-Q019` Atal Innovation Mission.
- `UPSC-CSE-2019-GS1-Q069` review of independent regulators.
- `UPSC-CSE-2026-GS1-Q065` NITI MPI → Economy.

### UPPCS

**A. NITI / Planning Commission status + legacy**
- `UPPCS-2018-GS1-Q015` — first Vice-Chairman, NITI Aayog.
- `UPPCS-2018-GS1-Q017` — NITI as extra-/non-constitutional body.
- `UPPCS-2018-GS1-Q092` — Planning Commission called “Economic Cabinet of India” by Ashok Chandra.

**B. NITI institutional role / structure**
- `UPPCS-2019-GS1-Q140` — NITI as nodal institution for SDG coordination.
- `UPPCS-2020-GS1-Q138` — DMEO attached to NITI Aayog.

**C. Inter-State Council**
- `UPPCS-2021-GS1-Q026` — Sarkaria Commission recommendation → establishment of ISC.

**D. Lokpal**
- `UPPCS-2024-GS1-Q130` — Lokpal Selection Committee membership.

**Semantic exclusions**
- `UPPCS-2018-GS1-Q063` poverty-line attribution → Economy.
- `UPPCS-2020-GS1-Q015` AIM → sibling agency/function node.
- NITI SDG/state-index/aspirational-district ranking questions → dynamic current layer.

### Demand synthesis
- **Recurring concepts:** institutional source/status; planning architecture; composition; appointment/selection; constitutional provision ↔ statutory Act mapping.
- **Recurring traps:** non-constitutional ≠ non-statutory; NDC ≠ Planning Commission organ; ISC ≠ Zonal Council; Lokpal ≠ constitutional body; “Chairperson can be CJI” ≠ “only CJI can be Chairperson.”
- **Qualifier patterns:** `how many`, `only`, `not`, `established as per Constitution`, `maximum`, `not less than`, `in/outside India`.
- **UPSC vs UPPCS:** UPSC → multi-body classification + qualifier-heavy statutory statements; UPPCS → direct institution-person/source/committee recall.
- **Recent reinforcement:** UPSC 2023 + two 2025 institutional-status/Lokpal questions; UPPCS 2024 Lokpal selection.

## 3. Base-Note Coverage
- **already strong:**  
  - **KEEP:** Article 263 anchor for Inter-State Council.  
  - **KEEP:** Zonal Councils = statutory; States Reorganisation Act, 1956.  
  - **KEEP/COMPRESS:** Zonal Council functions/objectives; Home Minister chair; rotating CM vice-chair. PRE__POL__BASE__SELF__2026__Pol…
- **partial:**  
  - **COMPRESS:** ISC composition + Standing Committee; retain durable structure, not current office-holders/names.
  - **PARTIAL:** Lokpal appears only incidentally through LoP selection-committee references.
- **missing:** dedicated NITI Aayog; Planning Commission; NDC; Lokpal composition/jurisdiction/eligibility; comparative constitutional–statutory–executive matrix.
- **redundant:** lengthy Zonal Council objectives; exhaustive current member-State/UT rosters.
- **outdated/suspect:**  
  - **CORRECT:** ISC Standing Committee numbers in base note.
  - **OMIT/VERIFY dynamically:** frozen Zonal Council State/UT rosters.
  - No dedicated base-note treatment of the core NITI/NDC/Lokpal PYQ cluster.

## 4. Core Fact Payload

### A. Classification key

| Category | Test |
|---|---|
| **Constitutional body/mechanism** | Source in Constitution itself → Article/provision anchor |
| **Statutory body** | Created by an Act of Parliament/State Legislature |
| **Executive / non-statutory body** | Created by executive resolution/order/notification; no constituting statute |
| **Non-constitutional** | Umbrella: body not created/provided by Constitution; may be **statutory or non-statutory** |

**Trap:** `non-constitutional` ≠ `non-statutory`.

### B. Status master-table

| Body | Creation / anchor | Status | PYQ hook |
|---|---|---|---|
| **NITI Aayog** | Union Cabinet Resolution, **1 Jan 2015** | Executive; non-constitutional; non-statutory | Replaced Planning Commission; policy think tank [NITI Aayog](https://www.niti.gov.in/about-us/niti-aayog-constitution/cabinet-secretariat-resolution-dated-01-01-2015?utm_source=chatgpt.com) |
| **Planning Commission** | GoI Resolution, **15 Mar 1950** | Executive; non-constitutional; non-statutory | Its 1950 Resolution was superseded by NITI Resolution w.e.f. 1 Jan 2015. [NITI Aayog](https://www.niti.gov.in/sites/default/files/2022-11/Cabinet_Secretariat_Resolution_dated_01-01-2015.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com) |
| **NDC** | Executive resolution, **Aug 1952** | Non-constitutional; non-statutory | Centre–State planning forum; not an organ of Planning Commission. BOTH__POL__BOOK__LAXMIKANTH__20… |
| **Lokpal** | Lokpal and Lokayuktas Act, 2013 | **Statutory** | Anti-corruption institution; statutory composition/jurisdiction. [India Code](https://www.indiacode.nic.in/handle/123456789/2122?col=123456789%2F1362\&utm_source=chatgpt.com) |
| **Inter-State Council** | **Article 263**; Presidential Order **28 May 1990** | Constitutional mechanism/body | Sarkaria recommendation; PM chairs. [Ministry of Home Affairs](https://www.mha.gov.in/sites/default/files/AnnualReport_15_16.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com) |
| **Zonal Councils** | Part III, States Reorganisation Act, 1956 | **Statutory**, not constitutional | Five councils; Union Home Minister chairs. [Ministry of Home Affairs](https://www.mha.gov.in/en/divisionofmha/centre-state-division/Centre-State-Matters?utm_source=chatgpt.com) |
| **National Security Council** | Executive governance framework; NSC Secretariat under Cabinet Secretariat/AoB Rules | Non-constitutional | In UPSC 2025 set, not Constitution-established. [Cabinet Secretariat](https://cabsec.gov.in/writereaddata/allocationbusinessrule/completeaobrules/english/1_Upload_3973.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com) |

### C. NITI Aayog

- Established **1 January 2015**; successor to Planning Commission; Cabinet resolution → neither constitutional nor statutory. BOTH__POL__BOOK__LAXMIKANTH__20…
- **Chairperson:** Prime Minister.
- **Core role:** apex policy think tank; directional + policy inputs; strategic/technical advice; cooperative federalism. [NITI Aayog](https://www.niti.gov.in/about-us/objectives-and-features?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- **Governing Council:** PM + CMs of States and UTs with legislatures + Lt Governors/Administrators of other UTs + specified NITI members/invitees. [NITI Aayog](https://www.niti.gov.in/divisions/division/governing-council-secretariat?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- Original organisational framework:
  - Vice-Chairperson → appointed by PM.
  - Full-time members.
  - Part-time members → max **2**.
  - Ex-officio Union Ministers → max **4**.
  - CEO → appointed by PM for fixed tenure; Secretary-to-GoI rank.
  - Regional Councils → issue/region-specific; specified tenure. [NITI Aayog](https://www.niti.gov.in/sites/default/files/2022-11/Cabinet_Secretariat_Resolution_dated_01-01-2015.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- **First Vice-Chairman:** Arvind Panagariya. [Press Information Bureau](https://www.pib.gov.in/newsite/PrintRelease.aspx?lang=2\&reg=48\&relid=114571\&utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- UPPCS-tested extensions: SDG coordination role; DMEO organisational linkage. NITI has officially been entrusted with coordinating SDGs. [NITI Aayog](https://www.niti.gov.in/node/381?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

### D. Planning Commission + NDC

**Planning Commission**
- Established March 1950 by executive resolution; advisory national-planning body. [NITI Repository](https://digitallibrary.niti.gov.in/items/ee45ad8c-86e1-4e56-90e0-c185d72a375c?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- **PM = Chairman**; States did not obtain NITI-style Governing Council partnership.
- Superseded by NITI Aayog from **1 Jan 2015**. [NITI Aayog](https://www.niti.gov.in/sites/default/files/2022-11/Cabinet_Secretariat_Resolution_dated_01-01-2015.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

**NDC**
- Set up 1952; independent Centre–State planning forum → **not an organ/subordinate body of Planning Commission**.
- Historical composition:
  - PM → Chairman.
  - Union Cabinet Ministers.
  - CMs of States.
  - CM/Administrators of UTs.
  - Planning Commission members; later reference texts associated NITI members. BOTH__POL__BOOK__LAXMIKANTH__20…
- Historical functions → consider/review National Plan; common social/economic policy; resource mobilisation; balanced development. Official Planning Commission records describe it as a Centre–State cooperation forum. [NITI Repository](https://digitallibrary.niti.gov.in/items/ebb7d89d-c3b1-43cc-b835-8382b16975d9?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- **Current functioning/meeting trivia → OMIT**; taxonomy excludes dynamic institutional-functioning layer.

### E. Lokpal — high-yield statutory payload

**Basis:** Lokpal and Lokayuktas Act, 2013.

**Composition**
- Chairperson + **maximum 8 Members**.
- **50%** of Members → Judicial Members.
- **Not less than 50%** of Members → from SC/ST/OBC/minorities/women. [India Code](https://www.indiacode.nic.in/bitstream/123456789/2122/1/201401.pdf)

**Chairperson eligibility**
- is/has been **CJI**; **OR**
- is/has been **SC Judge**; **OR**
- eligible eminent person under statutory criteria.
- Therefore: **“only CJI can be Chairperson” = false.** [India Code](https://www.indiacode.nic.in/bitstream/123456789/2122/1/201401.pdf)

**Disqualifying/eligibility trap**
- Chairperson/Member cannot be MP or member of State/UT Legislature.
- Must be **≥45 years** on assuming office. [India Code](https://www.indiacode.nic.in/bitstream/123456789/2122/1/201401.pdf)
- Term → **5 years or age 70, whichever earlier**. [India Code](https://www.indiacode.nic.in/bitstream/123456789/2122/1/201401.pdf)

**Selection Committee**
- PM — Chairperson.
- Speaker, Lok Sabha.
- Leader of Opposition, Lok Sabha.
- CJI **or SC Judge nominated by CJI**.
- Eminent jurist nominated by President on recommendation of preceding members.
- **Rajya Sabha Chairman is NOT a member.** [India Code](https://www.indiacode.nic.in/bitstream/123456789/2122/1/201401.pdf)

**Jurisdiction**
- Act expressly applies to public servants **in and outside India**. [India Code](https://www.indiacode.nic.in/bitstream/123456789/2122/1/201401.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- Covers present/former PM, Union Ministers, MPs, Groups A–D Central officials + specified Central bodies.
- Sitting PM **is not blanket-exempt**:
  - exclusions for international relations, external/internal security, public order, atomic energy, space;
  - otherwise full bench + ≥2/3 approval for initiating PM inquiry;
  - inquiry in camera. [India Code](https://www.indiacode.nic.in/bitstream/123456789/2122/1/201401.pdf)

### F. Inter-State Council vs Zonal Councils

| | Inter-State Council | Zonal Councils |
|---|---|---|
| Basis | **Article 263** + Presidential Order, 1990 | **States Reorganisation Act, 1956** |
| Status | Constitutional mechanism | Statutory |
| Number | One ISC | **Five** Zonal Councils |
| Chair | PM | Union Home Minister |
| Nature | Recommendatory/coordination | Advisory/coordination |
| Key origin | Sarkaria Commission recommendation → permanent ISC | Nehru proposal; statutory zonal framework |

- ISC current order implements principally Article 263(b)–(c); it has **not** been assigned Article 263(a)'s duty of inquiring/advising on inter-State disputes. [Ministry of Home Affairs](https://www.mha.gov.in/sites/default/files/AnnualReport_27122024.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- ISC membership: CMs of States + UTs with legislatures; Administrators of other UTs; Governors of States under President's Rule; six Union Cabinet-rank Ministers nominated by Chairman. [Ministry of Home Affairs](https://www.mha.gov.in/sites/default/files/AnnualreportEnglish_04102023.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- ISC Standing Committee: Home Minister + **4 Union Cabinet Ministers + 8 CMs** in the latest cited official constitution. [Ministry of Home Affairs](https://www.mha.gov.in/sites/default/files/AnnualreportEnglish_04102023.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- Zonal Councils: Northern, Central, Eastern, Western, Southern; statutory and advisory; Home Minister common Chairman; CM vice-chairmanship by rotation. [Ministry of Home Affairs](https://www.mha.gov.in/sites/default/files/AnnualReport_13_14.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

### G. UPSC “how many constitutional?” elimination set

| Body | Correct classification |
|---|---|
| **NCBC** | **Constitutional — Art. 338B**; constitutional status via 102nd Amendment, 2018. [NCBC](https://ncbc.nic.in/User_Panel/UserView.aspx?TypeID=1113\&lang=en\&utm_source=chatgpt.com) |
| **NHRC** | Statutory — Protection of Human Rights Act, 1993. [NHRC](https://nhrc.nic.in/about-the-organisation?utm_source=chatgpt.com) |
| **Law Commission of India** | Non-statutory; constituted by GoI notification. [Law Commission of India](https://lawcommissionofindia.nic.in/?utm_source=chatgpt.com) |
| **NCDRC** | Statutory — Consumer Protection Act, 2019, s.53. [NCDRC](https://ncdrc.nic.in/bare_acts/CPA2019.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com) |
| **ISC** | Constitution-based — Art.263 |
| **National Security Council** | Non-constitutional |
| **Zonal Councils** | Statutory — States Reorganisation Act, 1956 |

## 5. UPPCS Extension Layer
- **Arvind Panagariya** → first NITI Aayog Vice-Chairman.
- **Ashok Chandra → Planning Commission = “Economic Cabinet of India”** — preserve only as UPPCS direct-recall fact.
- **Sarkaria Commission → permanent Inter-State Council → Presidential Order, 1990.**
- **NITI → SDG coordination**; institutional role tested directly.
- **DMEO → NITI organisational ecosystem**; retain as one-line institution-link fact, not a detailed organisational note.
- Lokpal Selection Committee → PM + LS Speaker + LoP LS + CJI/nominee SC Judge + eminent jurist; **Chairman RS excluded**.

## 6. PYQ Trap Checklist
- **Non-constitutional ≠ non-statutory.**
- **NITI = executive/non-statutory**, not statutory/constitutional.
- **Planning Commission = executive**, not constitutional.
- **NDC = executive/non-statutory**; **NDC ≠ organ of Planning Commission**.
- **ISC: Article 263** ≠ **Zonal Councils: States Reorganisation Act, 1956**.
- **ISC constitutional basis** ≠ Zonal Councils statutory basis.
- **NCBC constitutional** ≠ NHRC statutory ≠ Law Commission non-statutory ≠ NCDRC statutory.
- Lokpal **statutory**, not constitutional.
- Lokpal applies to public servants **inside + outside India**.
- Lokpal Chairperson **CJI/SC Judge/eminent person** ≠ only CJI.
- Lokpal Member/Chairperson **≥45**, not “above 45”.
- Sitting PM **within Lokpal jurisdiction subject to exclusions/special threshold** ≠ complete immunity.
- Lokpal Selection Committee: **LS Speaker yes; RS Chairman no**.
- `50% judicial members` ≠ `not less than 50% representation from SC/ST/OBC/minorities/women`.

## 7. Correction / Verification Register

### CORRECT
- **Base note:** ISC Standing Committee = “Home Minister + 5 Cabinet Ministers + 9 CMs” → **Home Minister + 4 Union Cabinet Ministers + 8 CMs** in the latest cited official constitution/reconstitution. [Ministry of Home Affairs](https://www.mha.gov.in/sites/default/files/AnnualreportEnglish_04102023.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- **Base note:** hard-coded Zonal Council State/UT membership lists → **do not freeze**; retain five-council names + statutory basis + chair/vice-chair structure; political/territorial reorganisations make rosters time-sensitive.
- **Canonical `UPSC-CSE-2014-GS1-Q057`:** gateway stores option **B (1,3,4)**. Correct planning-era answer is **C (2,5): NDC + Parliament**. Official Planning Commission material records that the planning approach was considered/approved by NDC and then placed before Parliament; independent UPSC answer-key reproductions also record option C. [NITI Aayog Library](https://library.niti.gov.in/cgi-bin/koha/opac-retrieve-file.pl?id=21693847ed62d555a6d68bdda2acc512\&utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- **NITI/Planning Commission:** do not describe NITI merely as renamed Planning Commission → Planning Commission's 1950 Resolution was **superseded** and NITI separately constituted by 2015 Cabinet Resolution. [NITI Aayog](https://www.niti.gov.in/sites/default/files/2022-11/Cabinet_Secretariat_Resolution_dated_01-01-2015.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

### VERIFY
- None blocking.
- Any future use of **current office-holders, current Zonal membership rosters, current Lokpal vacancies/functioning, latest NITI attached-body structure** → reverify at production date.

### OMIT
- Current Lokpal performance/case/vacancy statistics.
- Current NITI index rankings and State positions.
- Aspirational District counts and year-specific programme rankings.
- Detailed current Zonal Council State/UT rosters.
- Poverty-line attribution question (`UPPCS-2018-GS1-Q063`) → Economy.
- AIM/FSDC/NIIF/regulator-function questions → sibling `Governance: Regulators, Agencies, Standards & Digital Identity` node.
- Detailed NDC “present functioning” speculation; retain only durable planning-era institutional facts.

## 8. Supplement Decision
`NO_SUPPLEMENT_NEEDED`

- Taxonomy scope covered.
- Relevant UPSC + UPPCS demand covered.
- Base-note gaps filled by current reference + primary/official verification.
- 2014 canonical answer anomaly explicitly corrected.
- No material unresolved fact requires test-series/topper supplementation.

### Demand synthesis

- **Recurring concepts:** constitutional article ↔ body; appointing/removing authority; reports; function boundaries; FC devolution formula; CAG–PAC chain.
- **Recurring traps:** `recommend ≠ decide`; `appointment authority ≠ removal authority`; `constitutional body ≠ statutory/executive body`; exact article-number matching.
- **Qualifier patterns:** *only*, *not*, *correctly/not correctly matched*, *how many*, multi-statement combinations.
- **UPSC:** narrower bodies, but deeper qualifier-heavy testing—especially FC criteria/recommendations and ECI/CAG functional limits.
- **UPPCS:** broader body coverage + direct article/composition/chairperson/appointment-removal recall.
- **Recent reinforcement:** UPSC 2023 + 2025 → 15th FC; UPPCS 2023–25 → article mapping, ECI/UPSC functions, FC chairpersons.
- **Direct recurring-PYQ variants worth covering:** FC criteria across successive commissions; CEC vs EC removal; UPSC vs SPSC appointment/removal; Art. 338/338A/338B/340 differentiation.

## 4. Core Fact Payload

### A. Constitutional map

| Body | Anchor | High-yield provision |
|---|---|---|
| **CAG** | Arts. **148–151** | appointment/removal; duties; form of accounts; audit reports |
| **Finance Commission** | Arts. **280–281** | constitution/functions; recommendations laid before Parliament |
| **UPSC/SPSC/JPSC** | Arts. **315–323**, Part XIV | composition, appointment/removal, functions, reports |
| **ECI** | Art. **324**, Part XV | superintendence/direction/control of specified elections |
| **NCSC** | Art. **338** | SC safeguards |
| **NCST** | Art. **338A** | ST safeguards |
| **NCBC** | Art. **338B** | **trap-only cross-reference** |
| **Backward Classes inquiry commission** | Art. **340** | **≠ NCBC constitutional anchor** |

---

### B. Finance Commission

- **Art. 280:** President constitutes FC every **5 years or earlier**; **Chairman + 4 other members**, all appointed by President.
- **Art. 280(2):** **Parliament**, not President, empowered to prescribe qualifications/manner of selection.
- **Finance Commission (Miscellaneous Provisions) Act, 1951**
  - Chairman → experience in **public affairs**.
  - Other 4 → selected from persons with:
    - HC judge/qualified for HC judgeship;
    - special knowledge of government finance/accounts;
    - wide financial + administrative experience;
    - special knowledge of economics. [Finance Commission India](https://fincomindia.nic.in/constitutional-provisions?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- **Core recommendations**
  - Union ↔ States share of divisible taxes = **vertical devolution**.
  - inter-se allocation among States = **horizontal devolution**.
  - principles of **grants-in-aid** under Art. 275.
  - augment State Consolidated Fund for Panchayats based on SFC recommendations.
  - same for Municipalities.
  - other matter referred by President in interest of sound finance.
- **Art. 281:** President lays FC recommendations + **explanatory memorandum on action taken** before **each House of Parliament**. Recommendations are recommendatory; do not encode “automatic acceptance” as a constitutional rule. [Finance Commission India](https://fincomindia.nic.in/constitutional-provisions?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- **Divisible pool trap:** shareable Union-tax proceeds ≠ cesses/surcharges; work with **net proceeds**, not gross tax collection.
- **Income distance:** poorer/lower per-capita-income States receive larger relative weight; equity criterion.

#### 13th–15th FC: horizontal-devolution criteria

| Criterion | 13th FC | 14th FC | 15th FC |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Population | 25% — **1971** | 17.5% — **1971** | 15% — **2011** |
| Area | 10% | 15% | 15% |
| Income/Fiscal Capacity Distance | **47.5% Fiscal Capacity Distance** | **50% Income Distance** | **45% Income Distance** |
| Fiscal discipline | 17.5% | — | — |
| Demographic change/performance | — | 10% Demographic Change | **12.5% Demographic Performance** |
| Forest | — | 7.5% Forest Cover | **10% Forest & Ecology** |
| Tax/fiscal effort | — | — | **2.5%** |

13th figures: [Finance Commission India](https://fincomindia.nic.in/asset/doc/commission-reports/13th-FC/english/13fcrengVol1.pdf) 14th figures: [Finance Commission India](https://fincomindia.nic.in/asset/doc/commission-reports/Fincom-commission-Legacy-of-trust.pdf) 15th figures: [Finance Commission India](https://fincomindia.nic.in/asset/doc/commission-reports/XV-FC-VOL-I-Main-Report.pdf)

#### Commission-specific PYQ payload

- **13th FC**
  - States’ vertical share: **32%**.
  - recommended GST design/implementation-related measures including compensation framework.
  - grants to local bodies = valid PYQ feature.
- **14th FC**
  - States’ share: **32% → 42%**.
  - did **not** recommend sector-specific grants as tested by UPSC 2015. [Finance Commission India](https://fincomindia.nic.in/asset/doc/commission-reports/Fincom-commission-Legacy-of-trust.pdf)
- **15th FC (2021–26)**
  - States’ vertical share: **41%**, **not 45%**.
  - tax/fiscal effort **reintroduced** as criterion.
  - ₹**4,800 crore** grants to incentivise improved educational outcomes.
  - ₹**45,000 crore** performance incentives for agricultural reforms. [Press Information Bureau](https://www.pib.gov.in/Pressreleaseshare.aspx?PRID=1693868\&lang=2\&reg=3\&utm_source=chatgpt.com)

---

### C. Election Commission of India

- **Art. 324:** ECI controls preparation of electoral rolls/conduct of elections to:
  - Parliament;
  - State Legislatures;
  - President;
  - Vice-President. [Legislative India](https://www.legislative.gov.in/static/uploads/2025/07/9114eab35ed1da6c31fa85a82e400b28.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- **Not ECI:** Panchayat/Municipal elections → State Election Commissions.
- Constitution does **not** make ECI a permanently “five-member” body:
  - CEC + such number of other ECs as President fixes.
- **Ordinary election disputes:** ECI does **not** adjudicate election petitions; parliamentary/state-legislature election petitions are tried by **High Courts** under RPA 1951. [India Code](https://www.indiacode.nic.in/bitstream/123456789/2096/9/A1951-43.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- ECI determines election schedule—not Union Home Ministry.
- ECI handles recognition/symbol disputes involving recognised political parties → UPSC 2017 trap.

#### Current statutory appointment framework — 2023 Act

- President appoints CEC + ECs.
- Eligibility → holding/has held post equivalent to **Secretary to Government of India** + integrity + knowledge/experience in management/conduct of elections.
- **Search Committee:** Law Minister + 2 members not below Secretary rank → panel of 5.
- **Selection Committee:** PM (Chair) + LoP Lok Sabha + Union Cabinet Minister nominated by PM.
- Term → **6 years or 65 years**, whichever earlier.
- no reappointment.
- EC → CEC: aggregate EC+CEC tenure cannot exceed **6 years**. [India Code](https://www.indiacode.nic.in/bitstream/123456789/19721/1/a2023-49.pdf)
- **CEC removal:** like SC judge.
- **Other EC removal:** only on recommendation of CEC.
- resignation → President. [India Code](https://www.indiacode.nic.in/bitstream/123456789/19721/1/a2023-49.pdf)
- Business → **as far as possible unanimous**; difference → **majority opinion**. [India Code](https://www.indiacode.nic.in/bitstream/123456789/19721/1/a2023-49.pdf)

---

### D. Comptroller and Auditor General of India

- **Art. 148:** appointed by **President by warrant under hand and seal**.
- Removal → same manner + grounds as **SC judge**.
- after office → ineligible for further office under Union/State government.
- office administrative expenses → **charged on Consolidated Fund of India**. [Comptroller and Auditor General of India](https://cag.gov.in/en/pages/single/2)
- **CAG DPC Act, 1971:** term **6 years or age 65**, whichever earlier. [Comptroller and Auditor General of India](https://cag.gov.in/en/pages/single/3?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- **Art. 149:** duties/powers prescribed by **Parliamentary law** → CAG DPC Act.
- audit domain includes government receipts/expenditure + specified authorities/bodies/government enterprises under applicable law.
- **Art. 150:** form of Union/State accounts prescribed by President **on advice of CAG**.
- **Art. 151**
  - Union reports → **President → Parliament**.
  - State reports → **Governor → State Legislature**. [Comptroller and Auditor General of India](https://cag.gov.in/en/pages/single/2)
- **Art. 279:** CAG certifies **net proceeds** of taxes/duties → FC linkage.
- **CAG → PAC:** PAC scrutinises appropriation/finance accounts and CAG reports.
- CAG ≠ pre-expenditure “controller” of the exchequer; Indian audit predominantly **post-expenditure/post-facto**.
- CAG ≠ prosecuting/judicial authority.
- **1976 departmentalisation of Union accounts:** CAG relieved of compilation/maintenance of **Union Government accounts**; audit role retained.

---

### E. UPSC / SPSC / Joint State PSC

| Feature | UPSC | SPSC | Joint State PSC |
|---|---|---|---|
| Anchor | Arts. 315–323 | Arts. 315–323 | Art. 315(2) onwards |
| Appointment | **President** | **Governor** | **President** |
| Term | 6 yrs / **65** | 6 yrs / **62** | 6 yrs / **62** |
| Removal | **President** | **President** | **President** |
| Annual report | President → Parliament | Governor → State Legislature | as prescribed for concerned framework |

- Constitution fixes **no exact number** of PSC members.
- As nearly as possible **½ members** of every PSC → at least **10 years** government service at appointment.
- Joint State PSC:
  1. legislatures of 2+ States pass resolutions agreeing;
  2. **Parliament by law** provides for Joint Commission. [India Code](https://upload.indiacode.nic.in/showfile?actid=AC_DH_62_1008_00005_00005_1561028196113\&filename=the_constitution_of_india.pdf\&type=actfile\&utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- **Art. 317:** misbehaviour removal → President after **SC inquiry**; President can directly remove on specified grounds such as insolvency, paid outside employment, infirmity. [Union Public Service Commission](https://www.upsc.gov.in/about-us/constitutional-provisions/article-317-removal-and-suspension-member-public-service-commission?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- **Art. 320**
  - conduct recruitment examinations;
  - recruitment methods;
  - appointments/promotions/transfers;
  - disciplinary matters and specified service claims. [Union Public Service Commission](https://www.upsc.gov.in/about-us/constitutional-provisions/article-320-functions-public-service-commissions)
- **Art. 321:** legislature may extend functions.
- **Art. 323:** annual reporting; cases of non-acceptance of PSC advice require explanatory memorandum.
- PSC advice ≠ automatically binding government decision.
- UPPCS-specific proposition: UPSC functions may flow from **Constitution + Parliamentary laws + executive rules/orders + established conventions**.

---

### F. NCSC / NCST

| Feature | NCSC | NCST |
|---|---|---|
| Article | **338** | **338A** |
| Composition | Chair + Vice-Chair + **3 members** | Chair + Vice-Chair + **3 members** |
| Appointment | President by warrant | President by warrant |
| Core constituency | Scheduled Castes | Scheduled Tribes |

- **89th Constitutional Amendment Act, 2003:** separated the earlier combined SC/ST Commission; Art. 338 retained NCSC + new Art. **338A** created NCST. [Legislative India](https://www.legislative.gov.in/static/uploads/2025/07/0c2366570aee86b57258a378ddf6dbc1.pdf)
- Common functions:
  - investigate/monitor constitutional + legal safeguards;
  - inquire into specific complaints;
  - advise/participate in socio-economic development;
  - report to President;
  - recommend safeguard/welfare measures;
  - civil-court powers during investigation/inquiry;
  - Union/States to consult respective Commission on major policy matters affecting SCs/STs. [Social Justice](https://socialjustice.gov.in/public/ckeditor/upload/59941731055382.pdf)
- State-related part of report → Governor → State Legislature.
- **Critical article trap:** NCBC = **Art. 338B**, not Art. 340. Art. 340 concerns appointment of a commission to investigate conditions of backward classes. [NCBC](https://ncbc.nic.in/User_Panel/UserView.aspx?TypeID=1113\&lang=en)

## 5. UPPCS Extension Layer

- **Finance Commission chairpersons**
  - 1st → **K.C. Neogy**
  - 11th → **A.M. Khusro**
  - 12th → **C. Rangarajan**
  - 13th → **Vijay L. Kelkar**
  - 14th → **Y.V. Reddy**
  - 15th → **N.K. Singh**
- **ECI historical recall**
  - 1st CEC → **Sukumar Sen**
  - 1st woman CEC → **V.S. Ramadevi**
- **PSC**
  - SPSC members appointed by Governor **but removed by President**.
  - Joint PSC ≠ created by UPSC/Governors alone → State resolutions + Parliamentary law.
  - SPSC functions extendable by **State Legislature**.
  - UPSC function-source PYQ → Constitution + Parliamentary laws + executive rules/orders + conventions.
- **CAG**
  - 1976 → accounting separated from auditing at Union level.
  - CAG reports → key source scrutinised by PAC.
- **Article-matching drill**
  - 148 → CAG
  - 280 → Finance Commission
  - 315 → PSC
  - 324 → ECI
  - 338 → NCSC
  - 338A → NCST
  - 338B → NCBC
  - 340 → Backward Classes inquiry commission
  - 360 → Financial Emergency
  - 323A → Administrative Tribunals

## 6. PYQ Trap Checklist

- Finance Commission **recommends** ≠ itself distributes/appropriates funds.
- FC **Chairman + 4** ≠ Chairman + 6.
- FC qualifications → **Parliament by law** ≠ President.
- FC report → **President lays before Parliament** ≠ NITI Aayog.
- 14th FC → **42%** ≠ 32%.
- 15th FC → **41%** ≠ 45%.
- 14th **demographic change** ≠ 15th **demographic performance**.
- 15th criteria include demographic performance + forest/ecology + tax/fiscal effort; **governance reforms/stable government** ≠ horizontal criteria.
- ECI → Parliament/State legislatures/P/VP **≠ local-body elections**.
- ECI controls schedule **≠ MHA**.
- ECI **≠ ordinary election-petition adjudicator**.
- CEC removal **≠ other EC removal**.
- ECI decisions → “as far as possible unanimous” **≠ unanimity mandatory**.
- CAG audits **≠ pre-sanctions expenditure**.
- CAG report scrutiny → PAC; **CAG ≠ prosecution/judicial authority**.
- SPSC appointment → Governor; **removal → President**.
- Joint PSC → State resolutions + Parliament **≠ UPSC-created body**.
- UPSC **≠ supervisor of SPSC**.
- NCSC 338 ≠ NCST 338A ≠ NCBC 338B.
- **Art. 340 ≠ NCBC's constitutional article**.
- constitutional body status ≠ every “commission” being constitutional.
